Adopting that globally (`*, ::before, ::after { box-sizing: border-box }` in the theme) is the fix by construction for the whole class, but it changes the rendered size of every padded/bordered element that sets a width or height, so it needs phase A's computed-style snapshot to review, not a drive-by edit. **Second instance, same week:** `CarouselVirtualContent`'s slides never carried the themed `.dx-carousel-item` class, so the gap model's `padding-inline-start` next to `flex-basis: 100%` made each virtual slide 352px in a 336px snapport and the browser stopped snapping them (`971e955`, fixed by declaring `box-sizing: border-box` inline in the primitive's shared slide style). Two instances make this a class: the global border-box rule is the construction. **Why:** the owner compared the carousel with shadcn's current docs (`/docs/components/base/carousel`) and the gap turned out to be theme-wide, not per-demo. shadcn's docs render the **Nova** style on ~95% of pages (`<ComponentPreview styleName="base-nova">` in `apps/v4/content/docs/components/{base,radix,aria}/*.mdx`, shadcn-ui/ui@98a1fe6); our theme was measured against the old un-prefixed pages and matches neither Nova nor Vega cleanly (radius ~2px tighter than both; Vega-like shadows, Nova-like density). **Mechanism gap (the bigger one):** shadcn has semantic role tokens (`--background`, `--foreground`, `--card`, `--popover`, `--primary(-foreground)`, `--secondary`, `--muted`, `--accent`, `--destructive`, `--border`, `--input`, `--ring`, `--chart-1..5`, `--sidebar-*`, `--radius` + derived radii; OKLCH; `.dark` override) defined once in `app/globals.css`, and a *style* (`registry/styles/style-nova.css`) only layers utility bundles on top -- two independent, swappable axes. We have no role layer: a numbered ramp (`--primary-color`, `-1..7`, `--secondary-color-*`, status colours; light/dark via the `var(--dark,…) var(--light,…)` space-toggle) referenced directly at ~424 sites in 64/72 component stylesheets, used inconsistently ("border" = 5 different steps; "surface" = all 8). Only focus ring and destructive are already semantic. **Upstream history (why the ramp exists):** DioxusLabs/components `315b62e` (2025-06-13, #51) created the theme with ad-hoc semantic names (`--background-color`, `--brighter-background-color`, `--muted-background-color`); `d414935` (2025-06-20, #66) renamed them to the numbered ramp, commit message "use more uniform names for the theme colors" -- naming hygiene in an early preview, not a rejection of roles. Worth keeping from upstream: the space-toggle mechanism (one declaration per token covers `data-theme`, `prefers-color-scheme` and non-browser renderers, no JS) and plain CSS (no Tailwind). **Plan:** (A) add `--dx-` role tokens with a shadcn-name fallback (e.g. `--dx-border: var(--border, <ramp step>)`) so a pasted shadcn theme works without colliding with a host app's own bare variables; repoint the ~424 sites by intended role, proven value-preserving with `playwright/computed-style-snapshot.spec.ts` (empty before/after diff); keep the ramp names as aliases for `dx components add` users. (B) Nova foundations: radius token swap (S), then card/button/input/badge/tabs/dialog/select shadow + density pass (M). (C) remaining components (L, high regression surface; axe colour-contrast re-run per the theme file's own rule). A switchable style (Nova/Vega presets) becomes a token-level choice only after (A). Research: `dev-docs/research/theme-2026-09-26/theme-vs-base-nova.md`, `shadcn-carousel-parity.md`. **2026-10-03 chart tie-in (rows 117-119):** after the chart parity round the palette is the **one deliberate shadcn difference left in the charts gallery** -- our `--dx-chart-1..8` are multi-hue (blue, orange, green, amber, pink) while shadcn's current `/charts` uses a blue ramp, so series 2+ read orange/green where shadcn's read a darker blue (geometry is identical). Phase A's role tokens (`--chart-1..5` with a shadcn-name fallback) make adopting shadcn's chart theme a token-level choice for the charts page. **Constraint:** the parity tests (`preview/src/{chart,polar,chart_tooltip}_parity.rs`, fixtures in `preview/tests/shadcn/`) and the chart/gallery specs must stay green through the migration -- they compare geometry, data and props, not colors, so a clean palette swap must not touch them; a red parity test means the repoint changed something it should not have. Research: `dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/visual-parity.md`. **2026-10-04 addendum -- phases A, B and global border-box LANDED** (`7299a4e` A; `256d7e8`, `99f00b1`, `0e6d3ac` B; `bcfba01` border-box; snapshot tooling `dd87a89`, `9f8399d`; size accounting `20e2fa4`; per-site evidence `dev-docs/research/theme-2026-10-03/{role-token-map.md,border-box.md,sites.tsv}`). **Counts corrected:** the ramp was referenced at **968** sites (842 CSS + 126 inline Rust style strings), not ~424, and 'border' was four distinct light/dark pairs. **Phase A (`7299a4e`)**: `--dx-<role>` tokens with shadcn-name fallbacks (`--dx-border: var(--border, <ramp step>)`), ramp names kept as aliases for installers; a site was repointed only if it resolves to the role's exact value in every mode it uses -- **884 refs repointed** (685 on base roles, 199 on `-alt`/`-subtle` review markers), 78 left `KEEP-phaseB`, sidebar's 6 local `--dx-sidebar-*` definitions deleted; applied mechanically (`tools/apply.py` from `sites.tsv`; `tools/gen.py` resolves every declaration old vs new in both modes: 0 mismatches, 0 REPOINT rows left). **Value-preservation proof:** the format-2 computed-style snapshot (light and dark, 37 colour properties, svg descendants, `::before/::after/::placeholder/::marker`, a `:focus-visible` pass) of 7299a4e vs unmodified 2241da8 shows **0 differences, light and dark**, once `9f8399d` made captures deterministic (A/A byte-identical): the focus pass looped ~200 calendar cells synchronously and hung the renderer to the 20-min timeout (now yields per element), two `setInterval` demos (progress width, home player's slider range) are frozen, and per-tree `#[css_module]` hashes and `dxc-<n>-` ids are normalised (without that, 140 false diffs). One early capture used stale HTML (the dx `--ssg` stale-public class, row 116 addendum) and was discarded. **Phase B reconciliation (`256d7e8`)**: the 170 remaining `-alt` uses and 72 ramp refs outside the Nova set moved to the semantically right shadcn role (every change with its before/after light/dark hex in role-token-map.md section 10, biggest visible deltas in 10.4); 6 `--dx-border-alt` hover/ring sites and toggle/toggle_group's `on` state kept on purpose. New gate **`scripts/check-css-vars-defined.sh`** (every `var(--x)` must be declared or carry a fallback) was **red on unmodified main**: five properties that never existed in git history -- `--primary-color-8` (navigation_menu featured gradient, so the card rendered transparent in dark), `--primary-color-9/-11/-12` (virtual_list demos, text silently inherited), `--toast-padding` (computed 0) -- all fixed. **Nova foundations (`99f00b1`)** at `shadcn-ui/ui@295a1f1` (`style-nova.css`, `globals.css`, `registry/bases/base/ui`), plain CSS and `--dx-` tokens, for card, button, input, badge, tabs, dialog, select: radius `var(--radius, 0.625rem)` with shadcn's multiplicative scale (every corner ~2px rounder, and a host `--radius` now drives it), 2rem controls, 3px 50% ring focus plus ring-coloured border, invalid ring at rest, cards/dialogs lose the drop shadow for a 1px foreground-10% ring, dialog 24rem left-aligned, tabs a 2rem muted pill, badges pills without the halo. **Cleanup (`0e6d3ac`)**: `--dx-border-alt` renamed **`--dx-border-strong`** at its 6 sites (the one non-shadcn colour role left, besides status roles) and all 11 `-alt` tokens deleted (verified unreferenced); the **radius gate restored** -- `check-css-literals.sh` skipped every token containing `var(`, so once the radius scale became `calc(var(--dx-radius) * k)` a literal `border-radius: 0.5rem` passed silently; it now resolves each `--dx-radius-*` step at the default base and fails if `--dx-radius` stops being `var(--radius, Nrem)` (it immediately caught pagination's literal `0.625rem`); InputGroup's wrapper focus/fill now matches Input; `dx-utilities.css` regenerated (`generate-dx-utilities.js --check` was STALE on HEAD, a red check). **Global border-box (`bcfba01`; third instance of the class after `09cd59e` and `971e955`: the charts card body) -- scoped, not Tailwind's bare `*`:** `:where([class^="dx-"]), :where([class*=" dx-"])` and their `::before/::after`, zero specificity. Why not `*`: shadcn can use `*` because Tailwind's preflight is a prerequisite of every shadcn install, but here the theme file is copied into every `dx components add` user's app, so `*` would resize THEIR padded/sized elements and every third-party widget the moment one component is added; the `dx-` scope touches only our own markup (every shipped class carries `dx-`, `check-dx-class-prefix.sh`, and `#[css_module]` hashing only appends a suffix), and zero specificity lets a component's own `box-sizing` and `all: unset` still win. By-design gaps (still content-box): unclassed inner wrappers and demo divs, `all: unset` elements (colour-picker thumbs, switch; `.dx-slider-thumb` keeps its own declaration), pseudo-elements other than `::before/::after`. ~24 rules change size (border-box.md section 3: each old -> new), two compensated (`.dx-form-result`, `.dx-top-layer-stack-area`), 78 redundant per-site declarations removed, the `09cd59e` demo workaround reverted; primitives keep theirs (they ship without the theme). **Verified, not predicted (`20e2fa4`):** the A -> tip snapshot showed 8 size changes the static list missed -- 5 intended border-box effects that match shadcn under preflight (carousel slide cards 320x320 -> 320x352 because the `aspect-ratio` square now includes CardContent's padding; scroll-area demo 194x178 -> 160x160; resizable group 452x322 -> 450x320; code-panel `max-height: 80vh` and sidebar demo iframe now exact; table rows 40.5 -> 40) and 3 probe artifacts (a pre- vs post-hydration home-page rect: select 153.8 -> 128, avatar, chart) -- no regressions, no CSS change. Reading rule: `getComputedStyle().width/height` is the content size under content-box, so every padded element looks 'resized' in the snapshot while its box did not move; check width/height lines against `getBoundingClientRect`, and make the probe wait for hydration. The extra x-axis tick label on two charts is phase B's 16px card padding widening the plot, not border-box. **Deliberately NOT taken (recorded in `99f00b1`):** shadcn's grey focus ring (owner decision, ~2.6:1 on white), Nova's tinted destructive button (`bg-destructive/10 text-destructive`, ~2.7:1 in dark; row 134), and the `#171717` dark card/popover (hover states elsewhere rely on the gap). **Still OPEN -- phase C (L):** (a) every component outside the seven Nova dirs gets its Nova density pass; (b) re-run axe colour-contrast over the docs (the theme file's own rule) -- not run this round; (c) `test-harness/assets/dx-components-theme.css` is a stale copy that differs from `preview/assets/dx-components-theme.css` (last touched by PR #34); (d) dark `--dx-primary` is the literal `#e5e5e5` (shadcn's `oklch(0.922)`; the ramp gave `#fafafa`), the one existing token value phase B moved -- check consumers that use it as ink in dark during (b); (e) 40 `var(--primary-color...)`/`var(--secondary-color...)` references remain (theme aliases, `sheet`, `carousel`, `toggle`, `toggle_group`, `main.css`, `top_layer.rs`; static grep at `d9bcfda`); (f) the chart palette (row 119 tie-in above) still waits on the owner; (g) rows 129, 132-135 are theme-adjacent findings from this round. Brief: `dev-docs/next-steps-2026-10-04.md`. **Suite/gate results for these commits:** full debug suite on `20e2fa4` (2026-10-04) 1414 passed / 32 failed / 13 skipped (28.4 min), no regressions: the 32 = known rows 113, 21, 121, 106, 114, the Rule 4c selftest (red by design), load flakes that were green on rerun, the pre-fix Navbar probe, and 5 specs updated to the theme's intended values (`b9e9580`); all gates green (913 cargo tests, fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, stylelint, every `scripts/check-*.sh` incl. `check-css-vars-defined.sh`, `generate-dx-utilities.js --check`); plus release no-base-path SSG build of `b9e9580` (`build-ssg.sh release`, 9m38s, STALE_HTML=0, 88 pages), chromium `ssg.local.config.ts`: `auto-popover-opening-gesture.spec.ts` 2/2 green (Navbar no longer toggles closed, DropdownMenu control green); `navbar.spec.ts` 5/5; `keyboard-matrix.spec.ts` 77 passed / 1 skipped; `top-layer.spec.ts` 90 passed / 8 skipped / 1 failed (Rule 8 ColorPicker :941, known row 106); `hydration-parity.spec.ts` 41 passed / 1 failed (Rule 4c selftest, red by design); `main-thread.spec.ts` 58/58; no new failures. **2026-10-04 round-2 addendum -- theme picker and shadcn presets landed; preset contrast sweep (uncommitted at time of writing).** (1) *Picker:* shadcn's customizer as data. `preview/assets/theme-presets.css` is GENERATED by `dev-docs/research/theme-2026-10-03/tools/gen_presets.mjs` from `shadcn-ui/ui@295a1f1` (`apps/v4/registry/themes.ts`: 7 base colours and 17 accents; `_legacy-base-colors.ts`: gray and slate) -> **9 bases x 17 accents x 6 radii** (0, 0.3, 0.5, 0.625, 0.75, 1 rem), OKLCH values verbatim. Three attributes on `<html>` (`data-theme-base`, `-accent`, `-radius`) compose by cascade exactly like shadcn's `{...baseColor, ...accent}` merge and set shadcn's BARE variables, which phase A's `--dx-<role>: var(--shadcn-name, <ramp>)` fallbacks already read, so every component recolours with no component edit -- the payoff phase A was built for; no attribute means the library's own look. A pre-paint snippet in `preview/index.html` reads cookies (`dx_theme_base`, `_accent`, `_radius`) so nothing flashes. `preview/src/theme.rs` holds the picker: a header popover on desktop and, at the header's <= 760px breakpoint, the same panel as a bottom `Drawer` opened from the mobile nav sheet (`ThemePickerSidebarFooter`); which trigger shows is CSS, not a Rust width check (that would render different markup before and after hydration). Swatches reuse the preset blocks, so the colour data exists once. Spec: `playwright/theme-preset.spec.ts`. Known gap: no phone entry on routes without the docs sidebar (row 142). (2) *Contrast sweep:* `playwright/theme-preset-contrast.spec.ts` reads the base and accent names from `theme-presets.css` (plus "none" per axis), applies every base x accent x light/dark, measures computed colours composited through a 1x1 canvas (so translucent fills count) and asserts >= 4.5:1 for the Badge and Button variants (rest and hover), the link Button, the Toggle and ToggleGroup states and the docs site's accent ink, plus a pressed toggle distinct from its hover and rest backgrounds. It forced three fixes, each explained in its stylesheet comment: the destructive Button/Badge fill (`--dx-destructive-fill`: solid in light, 60% over the page in dark, because shadcn's dark `--destructive` is a light salmon that carries the near-white foreground at 2.8:1; the comment cites >= 5.6:1 at 60% in every combination); the destructive Button hover (`color-mix(fill, foreground 8%)`, the Secondary button's move, replacing a 90%-alpha lightening that measured 4.35:1 light / 3.2:1 dark under shadcn's palette); the link Button ink (`--dx-primary-ink`: OKLCH lightness clamped to <= 0.5 in light and >= 0.75 in dark, because a preset's `--primary` is a fill and read as text it was 1.5:1 in places). **Phase C status:** this is a preset-contrast sweep over a component subset, NOT the axe colour-contrast pass over the docs that item (b) asks for, so (b) stays open; row 134's tinted destructive form is still deliberately not taken (these fixes cover the solid form only); the audit in row 145 adds AlertDialog (not on Nova: radius 10px, literal shadow, 32/24px padding) and the Command dialog's different surface token to phase C's list. Results of the sweep and the round's other gates: see the round-2 verification note in row 116's addendum. |

Instance 1, FIXED: `carousel.spec.ts` "a sustained constant wheel stream at the start boundary..." failed 15/15 at `22d4c95` (release SSG, `--workers=1 --repeat-each=15`) and passed 15/15 at `c2dd9d7` (debug SSG) -- an in-page MutationObserver trace showed the same release/spring-back sawtooth at both revisions; the test read the transform across CDP round trips while a spring-back was in flight. Fixed by dispatching both bursts and both reads in one synchronous in-page turn. Instance 2, FIXED 2026-09-26 (`d44a12d`; was OPEN): `carousel.spec.ts:687` "ArrowDown/ArrowUp page the vertical carousel..." fails ~1/5 under `--workers=2`, 5/5 green under `--workers=1`; not root-caused -- check it against row 110's smooth-scroll pre-scroll class first | done (instance 2 fixed `d44a12d`; the wheel-release note is moot since mode D, `863f8ac`) | **2026-09-28:** the `wheelSpent()` product note is moot -- the B3 wheel path it describes was removed when mode D replaced it (`863f8ac`, row 102). **2026-09-26 (audit batch): instance 2 FIXED** in `d44a12d` -- not row 110's class (`.focus()` and raw key presses never pre-scroll); `expectSnappedToBoundary`'s `toPass` retry paid a CDP round trip per attempt inside a fixed 3s budget. The whole poll now runs in-page on rAF (all 25 call sites). 20/20 at `--repeat-each=20 --workers=2`; the original ~1/5 rate did not reproduce on demand in this session. The `wheelSpent()` product note stands, pending the owner's approach-E decision. [Superseded by the 2026-09-28 note above: the owner chose mode D, which removed that wheel path.] **Product note (not a test issue):** `CAROUSEL_WHEEL_BOUNCE_JS`'s `wheelSpent()` plateau branch accepts a delta merely *equal* to the previous one, so any flat stream (a notched wheel, or a perfectly steady push) is classified as spent after two events and `bounceHome()` starts mid-push. Rarely visible with a real hand's jittery deltas. Moot if the non-loop edges move to bench approach E (the browser's own snap releases at lift, no release heuristic at all), which is the owner's current preference; otherwise require a strict decline. **Class:** two carousel tests have now asserted on an animated value read across await gaps -- the construction is to sample in-page (one synchronous turn, or an in-page rAF/MutationObserver trace), never via separate round trips. |
| 113 | `calendar.spec.ts:364` "a range's in-between day keeps its muted look while hovered" times out (90s) on every run -- likely date-dependent | **LANDED 2026-10-05 (round 3): RESOLVED, and not date-dependent** -- Found 2026-09-26 by the audit batch's full-suite run: 3/3 identical timeouts on the branch AND on unmodified main (`78cff69`); the locator `.dx-calendar-grid-cell[...]:not([data-disabled])` `.nth(4)` never resolves | S (diagnosis) | Not root-caused. Suspect the test builds a range relative to today's date, so on some calendar days the in-between cells it expects don't exist or are disabled. Pin the calendar's reference date in the demo or the test, rather than retrying. Full-suite context at the same run: 1307 passed / 5 failed / 12 skipped; the other four failures are the by-design `selftest:unfixed:id` and three CSS Anchor Positioning geometry tests this sandbox's Chromium cannot pass (row 106's class), all identical on unmodified main. **Landed 2026-10-05 (round 3):** the guess above did not hold. With the clock pinned to the day the row was filed and to three other days (2026-05-15, 2026-01-31, 2026-02-28) the locator resolved to all 31 May cells every time, because the `range` demo fixes its own `view_date` to 2026-05-15 and its min/max leave May 2026 available. What reproduces the 90 s timeout deterministically is the URL: the spec opened `/component/block/calendar/range/` (the path form), which only the dev server's SPA fallback serves; the static SSG server has no prerendered file for it and answers 404 (curl: 404 against 200 for the query form), so the calendar never rendered and the first `.click()` waited out the whole test timeout. **Fix, in `playwright/calendar.spec.ts`:** the query form `/component/block/?name=calendar&variant=range&` (both lanes serve it; every other block-page spec uses it), `page.clock.setFixedTime` to a day outside the demo's May 2026 view so `data-today` can never land on one of the cells, and a `toHaveCount(31)` precondition that fails in seconds with a message instead of burning the timeout. Lesson for row 164: a spec URL that 404s on the SSG build looks like a hang, so assert the page's own precondition first, and block demos have no prerendered path form. |
| 114 | `drag_and_drop_list.spec.ts` lifecycle tests (`:284` mouse drop to the side, `:300` cancelled mouse drag) compare an item's text captured before its avatar fallback initials render with one read after -- fails under parallel load | **LANDED 2026-10-05 (round 3)** -- Found 2026-09-26 by the audit batch's integration run (`--workers=4`): 1 failure in the full suite; isolated, 7-8 failures in ~100 runs at `--repeat-each=6 --workers=4`, 0/8 at `--workers=1`. **Pre-existing:** `78cff69`'s own spec against `78cff69`'s own build fails the same way (8 failed / 94 passed) | S | Received texts end in the avatar initials the expected text lacks (`...Apr29` vs `...Apr29KT`): the test snapshots `itemText()` while the avatar is still on its image path, and the fallback text arrives later. Fix the comparison, not the timing: identify items by a stable key (the task id such as `LNC-147`, or a `data-` attribute) rather than their whole `textContent`, or wait for the avatars to settle before the first read. Same family as row 112: an assertion comparing two reads of a value that is still changing. **Landed 2026-10-05 (round 3):** fixed in the comparison, not the timing, as this row proposed. `itemText` in `playwright/drag_and_drop_list.spec.ts` now clones the item and drops the `.dx-avatar` subtree before reading `textContent`: the avatar is decoration, not part of an item's identity, and what remains (title, task code, status, due date) is stable from the first render, so nothing waits for the avatars to settle. 45/45 repeats pass (the 7-8 failures in ~100 runs at `--repeat-each=6 --workers=4` are gone). Related: the demo avatars are now local geometric SVGs (`scripts/gen-demo-avatars.mjs`, gate `check-demo-remote-assets.sh`), so no third-party image is on the page to wait for. |
| 115 | `tooltip.spec.ts:6` "tabbing out of the trigger element should hide the tooltip" failed once in a full-suite run -- not reproduced | Seen 2026-09-28 in the mode-D integration run (`--workers=4`, SSG release build): the tooltip was still present 5s after `Tab` (`toHaveCount(0)` received 1). Re-run immediately: `tooltip.spec.ts` `--repeat-each=6 --workers=4`, 180/180 passed | S (diagnosis, when it recurs) | **2026-10-01: DIAGNOSED** (`e698054`, `58a8e36`) -- not one bug: one product race plus three test-methodology classes. (1) tooltip:6 / hover-card:6 were a real race: `use_popover_shown_while_mounted` mirrored `toggle(open)` -- the async echo of its own `showPopover()` -- back into the signal, so a blur/mouseleave landing before that task left the popover stuck open; reproduced deterministically by blurring inside `showPopover()`; fixed by forwarding only native closes; regression tests in both specs. (2) `dx-class-migration` (5 failures in ~1400 runs): measured before hydration, the body fallback picked up the navbar's `dx-select-expand-icon` -- row 109's class, fixed with `gotoHydrated` (also applied to the tooltip/hover-card/drawer specs). (3) close-fade tests (hover-card:57, tooltip, popover): `toBeVisible()` passes at opacity 0 and a frame-count threshold is load-dependent -- row 112's class; one entry point `sampleCloseFade` with an event-based "fades, not snaps" check plus rate-independent invariants. (4) drawer:110 (inferred, fixed 500ms wait) -> shared `awaitAnimationsSettled`; drawer:140 read state after a backdrop-dismissing release -> read before `mouse.up()`. Verified 870/870 and 210/210 at x30 under mixed load, 250/250 at 25x CPU throttle. **Still open:** `combobox.spec.ts:134` (~10% under load on main too; same transient-window class, needs in-page sampling); `menu-submenu.spec.ts:307` hover-intent delay (load-sensitive on main too); carousel timing tests under heavy load -- autoplay racing setup, rapid paging, and `virtual_many` "Previous/Next are genuinely disabled at the real, non-looping ends" (4/6 failures on main in isolation, off-by-one counts); product: a drag released over the drawer's `::backdrop` dismisses it even when dragged away from the dismiss direction; `use_popover_sync` likely has the mirror-image stale-`toggle(closed)` hazard (unproven). **New instance, same day:** the new `sampleCloseFade` helper itself flaked once in the integration full suite (`popover.spec.ts:238`, `--workers=4`): 4 samples over 58ms, opacity 1, no animation started -- either the close trigger had not taken effect yet or the sampling window ended too early; 80/80 on an immediate `--repeat-each=10 --workers=4` rerun. Next step: make the helper wait for the close to begin (e.g. `data-state` flip or `toggle` event) before judging the fade. **2026-09-30 addendum -- two more one-offs of the same shape:** in the carousel true-end integration run (`--workers=4`, SSG release build, 1373 passed / 8 failed), `dx-class-migration.spec.ts:162` "tabs: stylesheet delivered, classes unhashed" and `hover-card.spec.ts:6` each failed once; on a rerun of both files `--repeat-each=8 --workers=4` each failed 1 of 8, the other rows passing (`drawer.spec.ts:110`, also red once in the full run, passed 8/8). None load carousel code, and that batch changed only carousel code, demos, docs and specs. Three overlay/stylesheet specs flaking only under parallel load is a pattern, not three coincidences: next step is one diagnosis lane across all three. Not root-caused, and unrelated to the carousel change in that run. If it recurs, check whether a hover left over from Playwright's own pre-scroll or a neighbouring frame re-opens the tooltip after the focus-out close, and sample the tooltip's open state in-page across the `Tab` rather than via `toHaveCount` polling. |
| 116 | Build/dev loop efficiency: release SSG build takes ~18 min; lanes rebuild cold | **LANDED 2026-10-04** (steps 1, 2-3 via scripts, 5; step 4 dropped; step 6 timeout only) -- was: **Open -- owner-requested 2026-10-01; deferred until after the carousel visual fixes** | M | **Measured 2026-10-01 (not assumed):** (a) `scripts/deploy-preview.sh` (release, `--ssg`, fullstack, `--force-sequential=true`) took **1069 s** on this 4-core sandbox, building `fec73f0`. (b) `[profile.release]` in the workspace `Cargo.toml` is `opt-level = "z"`, `lto = true`, `codegen-units = 1`, and it applies to the SSG *server* binary too -- which only prerenders the static pages and is never shipped. (2) **Verification lanes use debug SSG builds** (`ssg.local.config.ts` already supports the `debug` output dir); release only for `deploy-preview.sh` plus one pre-merge release run. (3) **Lanes start from a copied warm, base-path-free target dir** (as `lane-cp` did), never cold; prune stale target dirs (row 100 for why the copy must be base-path-free). (4) **lld as the linker** for the host/server target. (6) **Loop:** real-device feel questions go through the telemetry lab artifact (`labs/carousel-lab`) -- the fastest loop this session; batch visual findings; run the full suite once pre-merge, chromium only; consider a shorter per-test timeout so failures surface sooner. **Measured follow-up 2026-10-01 (full suite on main `4f0f12b`):** a debug SSG full run (chromium, 4 workers, 23.9 min) gave 1391 passed / 28 failed / 12 skipped; 23 failures repeat with `--workers=1`. Build times on the same isolated dir: debug SSG 8m29s, release SSG 1024 s cold (all 640 wasm + 714 server crates). Disk dropped to 56 MB free during that release link; free space is ~3.9 GB after pruning, so step (3)'s pruning is now urgent. **Measured follow-up 2026-10-03 (chart-parity session, PRs #79-#81):** (a) a fresh container lacked both `dx` and the `wasm32-unknown-unknown` target: `cargo install dioxus-cli --version 0.7.9 --locked` took ~21 min; (b) `dx`'s own tool downloader failed TLS (`invalid peer certificate: UnknownIssuer` -- it ignores the proxy CA bundle), so esbuild and binaryen/wasm-opt had to be seeded by `curl` into `/root/.local/share/.dx/tools/` before any build (same trap as the 2026-09-04 and 2026-09-18 environment notes -- it is now a third occurrence, so step (5)'s SessionStart hook should do the seeding, not a human); (c) release SSG builds took ~11-13 min **warm** (vs 1024-1069 s cold above); (d) concurrent lanes in one target dir hit `Text file busy` on the produced binaries and reused stale cross-tree artifacts (a mirror tree picked up the main tree's compiled primitives) -- the row-98/100 isolation rule, hit again; (e) the disk filled with ~18 GB of lane target dirs (five isolated dirs) twice in the session; (f) timing specs (`oracle/tier2-html/main-thread.spec.ts`, anything with a latency threshold) need a release build -- debug gives false reds, as measured above; (g) `scripts/deploy-preview.sh` ignores `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` (row 122) and `playwright/oracle/hydration-parity.spec.ts` hard-codes `:8090` (row 123) -- both belong in this row's step (3) when it is taken. Next-thread brief: `dev-docs/next-steps-2026-10-03.md` (this row first, then row 111). **2026-10-04 addendum -- LANDED** (`34d8532`, `8c99edd`, `7ad8421`, `832dbaf`, `150e97b`; measured on 4 cores, rustc 1.97.0, dx 0.7.9, one cargo at a time, isolated absolute `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`s; before/after table and raw logs: the subsection after this table, `/home/user/baseline/{REPORT,RELEASE,VERIFY}.md`). **Per step:** (1) DONE `34d8532` -- dx 0.7.9 builds the SSG server under `server-release` and the shipped wasm under `wasm-release` (dioxus-cli `platform.rs`, `build/request.rs`), both synthesised as `inherits = release`; a user-defined `[profile.server-release]` wins (confirmed via `cargo build --profile server-release --unit-graph`: opt-level 1, no LTO, 16 CGUs, incremental), while `wasm-release` is deliberately undefined so dx keeps its own opt-level `s` on the client (the workspace `z` never reached the wasm). (2)+(3) DONE as scripts: debug SSG for functional specs (works only since `7ad8421`, below); `scripts/lane-target.sh create\|prune\|du` (`8c99edd`) copies a warm base-path-free template into an isolated absolute dir per lane and refuses a template carrying a base-path build (row 100) -- measured by the build lane, a 6.1 GB template copies in 24 s and `cp --reflink=auto` silently degrades to a full copy on this filesystem (no reflink), so budget the disk; `deploy-preview.sh` honours `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` (rows 122/123). (4) DROPPED: rustc 1.97 already links `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` with its bundled `rust-lld` (stated in `34d8532`), so selecting `/usr/bin/lld` has nothing left to win. (5) DONE `832dbaf` -- `scripts/session-setup.sh`, run by a SessionStart hook only when `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE=true`: prebuilt dx 0.7.9 release binary (`cargo install --locked` fallback), wasm32 target, esbuild/binaryen/wasm-bindgen seeded by `curl` into dx's tools dir (TLS stays verified; the third occurrence of the downloader-ignores-the-proxy-CA trap), `npm ci` in `playwright/` and `preview/` (`preview/package-lock.json` force-added, the root `.gitignore` ignores lockfiles), `cargo fetch`; **measured 7.7 s from scratch (dx and tools removed), 0.94 s as a no-op**, versus ~21 min for `cargo install` alone. (6) TIMEOUT ONLY: per-test timeout 300 s -> 120 s (`PW_TEST_TIMEOUT` overrides) and `main-thread.spec.ts` ignored on known-debug builds (`PW_BUILD_PROFILE`; `PW_FORCE_TIMING_SPECS=1` overrides) in `8c99edd`; 'one full suite pre-merge' stays a convention, not a mechanism. **Result:** release SSG cold 1166 -> 904 s (-22 %), warm 723 -> 411 s (-43 %); the server phase carries the win (cold 582 -> ~393 s, warm 371 -> 76 s), the client-phase delta (584 -> ~511 s cold) is machine noise because the client profile is untouched. **Artifact identity (honest verdict):** `8c99edd`'s release output vs the 2241da8 baseline is NOT byte-identical but is identical after masking the four path-derived strings (preview js/wasm hash in `<link>`/`<script>`, `dxc-<n>` SSG request-order id counters, the base64 `initial_dioxus_hydration_data` blob that embeds them, the `#[css_module]` hash suffix): 0 residual differences across all 89 differing html/css/js files, 262 files each, same name set bar the hashed wasm/js names. The wasm differs by 2721 B = 544 embedded `/home/user/wt-<name>` source-path strings x 5 + 1 length byte, and a warm rebuild in the same worktree reproduced the cold wasm byte-for-byte (sha 3e658fd5...). **Not proven byte-for-byte at equal path** (would need a build at a path of identical length to the baseline worktree, ~9 min of client build); the client profile is untouched, so a profile effect is implausible. Side finding: same commit at a different worktree path never yields identical wasm/css names -- compare with masking (`baseline/identity.py`). **Two classes found on the way, both fixed by construction.** (i) *dx `--ssg` stale-public* (row 98/100 family, 'a build whose output does not match its inputs'): dx 0.7.9 `--ssg` rewrites only `public/index.html` when `public/` already exists, every other pre-rendered route keeps the previous build's HTML and its stale stylesheet hashes -- it produced false 'content-box regressions' during the border-box verification and was a third instance after rows 98 and 100. Construction (`150e97b`): `scripts/build-ssg.sh [debug\|release] [--base-path P]` is the only place that runs the SSG build; it requires an absolute `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`, refuses a no-base-path build into a `.base-path-build` tree, wipes the profile's `public/`, runs dx with deploy's flags, then fails unless every `public/**/index.html` is newer than the build start and the URLs match the requested base path; tested against a stub dx over 14 cases; `deploy-preview.sh` calls it. (ii) *every debug wasm build of `preview` failed to link on rustc 1.97* (`rust-lld: error: ... libarborium_tree_sitter ...: undefined symbol: stderr`; cold debug SSG 812 s then exit 1, plain CSR build 326 s exit 1 -- `baseline/failed-debug-attempts/`), which had silently blocked step (2). **Root cause:** dioxus-code's `lang-css` pulls tree-sitter's C runtime into the client (the debug Style tab highlights CSS in the browser); arborium 2.17.0's wasm libc shim lacks the *data* symbol `stderr` that `ts_stack_print_dot_graph` (stack.c) reads; rustc no longer passes `--allow-undefined` for wasm32-unknown-unknown; release links only because lld dead-strips that unreachable function. **Fix (`7ad8421`):** define the one missing symbol in `preview`; linking `arborium-sysroot` instead duplicates every other shim symbol, and `--allow-undefined` (the baseline lane's `wasm-ld` shim) hides real link errors and breaks the x86 server build under `--ssg` (`cc: unrecognized option`); verified in an isolated repro crate (debug and release link strict). Tip release build `9f8399d` (no shim, no base path): 916 s, 88 pages with 0 `/dioxus-components/` references, no `undefined symbol: stderr`, wasm +2550 B vs baseline (path term -2176 B, so ~+4.7 KB real growth: the symbol plus theme changes). Upstream follow-ups: rows 130, 131. **Release-build Playwright on `9f8399d`:** `main-thread.spec.ts` 58/58; probe `auto-popover-opening-gesture.spec.ts` 1 red (Navbar, row 40) / 1 green (control); `color-picker.spec.ts` 'clicking outside' 50/50 (row 29); hydration-parity 41/1 (row 123). **Full suite and gates on the final tip:** full debug suite on `20e2fa4` (2026-10-04) 1414 passed / 32 failed / 13 skipped (28.4 min), no regressions: the 32 = known rows 113, 21, 121, 106, 114, the Rule 4c selftest (red by design), load flakes that were green on rerun, the pre-fix Navbar probe, and 5 specs updated to the theme's intended values (`b9e9580`); all gates green (913 cargo tests, fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, stylelint, every `scripts/check-*.sh` incl. `check-css-vars-defined.sh`, `generate-dx-utilities.js --check`); plus release no-base-path SSG build of `b9e9580` (`build-ssg.sh release`, 9m38s, STALE_HTML=0, 88 pages), chromium `ssg.local.config.ts`: `auto-popover-opening-gesture.spec.ts` 2/2 green (Navbar no longer toggles closed, DropdownMenu control green); `navbar.spec.ts` 5/5; `keyboard-matrix.spec.ts` 77 passed / 1 skipped; `top-layer.spec.ts` 90 passed / 8 skipped / 1 failed (Rule 8 ColorPicker :941, known row 106); `hydration-parity.spec.ts` 41 passed / 1 failed (Rule 4c selftest, red by design); `main-thread.spec.ts` 58/58; no new failures. **2026-10-04 round-2 addendum -- `scripts/run-gates.sh`, the one gate entry point.** CLAUDE.md's inline list of gates is replaced by `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=<absolute isolated dir> scripts/run-gates.sh`, because a list a human re-types is how row 139 happened. It runs, in CLAUDE.md's old order, every `scripts/check-*.sh` (now including `check-eager-head-document.sh`, `check-uncleared-intervals.sh` and `check-blocking-scroll-listeners.sh`), `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, three clippy arms, `cargo test --workspace`, then stylelint (`npx --no-install`). **Why three clippy arms (found 2026-10-04):** `cargo clippy --workspace` with no `--features` lints only the `not(feature = "web")` arm of primitives (104 `feature = "web"` cfg sites) and none of preview's `feature = "server"` code, yet both are what ships: `dx build --ssg --features fullstack` compiles the client with `web` and the prerender server with `server` (which implies primitives/web). Arms: `clippy-default` (`--workspace`, the not(web)/native arm), `clippy-web` (`-p dioxus-primitives -p preview --features dioxus-primitives/web,preview/web`), `clippy-server` (`-p preview --features server`), each `--all-targets -- -D warnings`, a superset of the old `--tests --examples`. `--all-features` is unusable instead: preview's `desktop` needs webkit2gtk/gtk (absent in the remote container) and it would switch `web` on everywhere, hiding the `not(web)` arm. **Guards by construction:** the target dir must be absolute and not the repo-root `target/` (rows 98/100); a clippy log containing a "generated N warnings" summary is red even at exit 0 (free to check; proven NOT to be the navbar miss's cause, row 139); the summary prints HEAD and a hash of the uncommitted diff, so a green names its own tree; a `scripts/check-*.sh` missing from the list fails the run with exit 2; exit 0 = every gate ran and passed, 1 = a red gate, 2 = usage or environment, 3 = green but PARTIAL (`--only`, `--skip` or `--stop-on-fail` hid gates), so a partial run cannot be mistaken for the full gate; `--list` prints the gate names. **Verification of the round-2 tree (2026-10-04, `44a103e` + the uncommitted round-2 diff; gates, release no-base-path SSG build, full suite, scroll re-measure on release): green after one real fix.** *Gates:* `run-gates.sh` ALL GREEN, 23/23 (947 cargo tests, three clippy arms, stylelint), diff-hash `63cf2ec50538`; again after the fixes below, diff-hash `98e08d3c4c41` (taken before this text and the deploy's `docs/` output were added). *Build:* `build-ssg.sh release`, cold isolated dir, no base path: 14 m 14 s (client 480 s, server done at 854 s), 93 pages (88 + the 5 chat-kit pages), STALE_HTML=0, 0 `/dioxus-components/` references; all 93 `<head>`s carry the theme pre-paint script and the anchor-settle script byte-identical to `preview/index.html`, both before the first stylesheet, no CSP, and link `theme-presets` and `dx-effects` CSS (and both scripts run: theme-preset, anchor-landing green). *Full suite* (chromium, `ssg.local.config.ts`, 4 workers, release, 1786 tests, was 1459 at `20e2fa4`): run 1 on the tree as handed over **1729 passed / 44 failed / 13 skipped** (22.1 m). **One real regression: `.dx-component-variant { content-visibility: auto }` (scroll lane, research section 7.4).** A skipped subtree has no layout box, so carousels and charts that measure on mount measured 0 inside skipped variants and never recovered: 33 tests red (`carousel-virtual-wheel` 18, `carousel` 7, `chart_tooltip` `default_index` 8), 30 on a serial rerun. Bisected on copies of the built CSS: all `content-visibility` off 51/51 green, only the variants rule on brings them back, cards and widget cards alone do not. Fix: the variants rule is removed from `preview/assets/main.css` (home cards and widget cards keep theirs: every carousel and chart card scrolled into view measures identically on the pre-round and the fixed build) and `scroll-main-thread.spec.ts` test 6 asserts the opposite (variants `visible`); if variants ever get the skip back, those demos must re-measure on un-skip first (row 143's `VirtualList` cv mode has the same hazard). Spec fixes for races the round exposed: `drawer.spec.ts` :245 and :286 pressed the handle while the panel was still sliding in (after the scrim change the slide-in lands ~100-150 ms later relative to `data-state=open`: 6/6 green on the pre-round build, 1-4 of 8 after; now `awaitAnimationsSettled`, 72/72 over 6 repeats); `navigation_menu.spec.ts`'s new Panel-layout block used a plain `goto` (the featured title read 16px/400, its pre-theme default, in ~10 % of runs at 4 workers; now `gotoHydrated`, 228/228 over 12 repeats, which also had :128 (row 121) green 12/12); `top-layer-ink.spec.ts` 'popover (modal arm)' still looked for the old 'Delete Item?' heading (the popover lane's Dimensions demo: now `.dx-popover-title`). Run 2 on the rebuilt tree (`build-ssg.sh release` again, 36 s, only the CSS asset changed): **1761 passed / 12 failed / 13 skipped** (20.9 m); serial `--workers=1` rerun of the 12: 6 green (load flakes: alert-dialog and dialog 'Scrim', `avatar.spec.ts:5`, `carousel.spec.ts:3389`, `message_scroller.spec.ts:169`, `main-thread.spec.ts:807` drawer (drag), which is 5/5 green alone on the new build and 4/5 on the pre-round one) and 6 are the known set (`calendar.spec.ts:364` row 113, `date-picker.spec.ts:73` and `:144` row 21, `top-layer.spec.ts:956` Rule 8 row 106, `drag_and_drop_list.spec.ts:300` row 114, the Rule 4c selftest). No new failure; green on the new surface: hydration-parity incl. the chat-kit and message_scroller pages, stylesheets-present, anchor-landing, scroll-main-thread, theme-preset (+ phone drawer), theme-preset-contrast, message_scroller, menu-indicator-gap, the menu specs, every scrim test, resizable (incl. touch), data_table, navigation_menu, `main-thread` (58/58 serial). *Scroll* (`node scripts/measure-scroll.mjs`, 1280x800, median of 3, release, before = the frozen pre-round build): idle 178 -> 45 main ms/s, idle layouts 71 -> 3 per 3 s, scroll main busy 6,745 -> 2,089 ms, layouts 387 -> 170, recalcs 1,181 -> 685, tasks > 16 ms 8 -> 0 (none > 50 ms either side), wheel p50/p95/max 41.2/57.8/79.5 -> 30.5/49.0/63.7 ms, live `setInterval` at the end 74 -> 0; with a 150 s warm-up the before build grows (idle 346 ms/s, 99 layouts, 160 intervals) and the fixed build does not (42 ms/s, 3 layouts, 0 intervals). Full table: `research/scroll-jank-2026-10-04.md` section 7.6.** |
| 117 | Chart display + docs UX round: series colors were black/grey/invisible on every SSG page, plus a docs-site UX sweep (PR #79) | **LANDED 2026-10-03 -- MentalGear/dioxus-components#79 (merged `98c430f`).** Reported by the owner against the deployed site: every chart drew black bars, grey areas and invisible lines | M | **Root cause (the headline defect):** `ChartContainer` emitted its `--color-<key>` rule as dynamic text inside a `<style>` element; Dioxus SSR prefixes dynamic text with a hydration marker, so the shipped CSS was `<style><!--node-id444-->[data-chart="dxc-394"]{...}` and Chromium parsed it as the selector `node-id444-- > [data-chart=...]` (confirmed via `sheet.cssRules`), which never matches -- `--series-color` was invalid and marks fell back to `fill: black; stroke: none`. Second live instance of the same class: `virtual_list`'s `random_heights` demo lost its first CSS rule the same way. **By construction:** the colors are an inline `style` attribute on the container's own element (attribute values never get hydration markers; caller style folded in after, so it can still override); the four demos that did `style { {CONST} }` use a string-literal child; new gate **`scripts/check-raw-text-interpolation.sh`** fails on any rsx `style {}`/`script {}` (also `noscript`, RCDATA `textarea`, path-qualified names) with an interpolated child -- reports all 5 sites on the old tree, passes now. **Also fixed in the same round:** pie/radial arcs were one near-black (a `chart-arc` rule's `fill: var(--series-color, currentColor)` overrode the per-arc fill attribute; rule removed); the visually hidden data table is wrapped in a clamped `chart-data-wrapper` (a table box cannot shrink below min-content and widened phone pages to 418-727px); tooltip values were invisible (color was `--primary-color`, the page background: 1.00:1 light / 1.06:1 dark; now 18.9:1 / 13.4:1); axis ticks had no `text-anchor` (x = middle, y = end + central baseline, offsets within 0.5px; `direction="ltr"` pinned on the svg root); SVG text had no fill rule and computed black in both themes (a zero-specificity `:where()` base rule themes it; arc labels get a paint-order stroke halo, 12.5:1 dark / 18.9:1 light); chart-id fragments (`safe_fragment_id`, now injective -- `'a%'` and `'a_25'` collided). **Intermediate constructions that were wrong and replaced (kept as the record, superseded by row 119):** a measured-width logical size, then a fixed viewBox with a counter-scaled `--dx-chart-text-scale` (clamp 1..2, then 0.5..2.5) -- an independent review showed caller-authored lengths (radii, bar radius, label insets) stayed in props units and donuts collapsed to 1px hairlines at 390; row 119's 1-unit-equals-1-CSS-px sizing removed the whole class. **Docs UX (same PR):** new `ComponentCategory::Charts` sidebar group (all 8 chart pages; most used to hide in the Data display fallback), chart docs rewritten for library users (real props/defaults, stale claims corrected against the code, samples that did not compile fixed), `build.rs` enables pulldown-cmark tables/strikethrough/task lists (only `ENABLE_GFM` was set, so pipe tables shipped as raw `\|` text; tables wrapped in a scroll container); **base-path-safe links** (docs.md used root-absolute `/component/?name=x`, which 404s on Pages; now canonical `/component/<name>/` prefixed with `router().prefix()`) guarded by new gate **`scripts/check-internal-hrefs.sh`** (fails on root-absolute `href` literals in preview `.rs` and the legacy `?name=` form; navigation_menu's demo hrefs were the second instance); phone header one 54px row at 390 (was 3 rows, 151px) and 16px gutter (was 0 at <=760px), `<=340px` header steps (0 pairwise overlaps at 300-390), footer stacks at <=760px, Resizable thumbnail clipped on phones, demo frames use 12px at <=480px (the rule never applied: `tabs/style.css` loaded later at equal specificity), `minmax(0,1fr)` page-header grid so the inline command ellipsizes, `scrollbar-gutter: stable` set statically on `html` (CLS 0.115 -> 0.0003 on `/component/chart/` at 390), dark link blue `#0065ff` -> `#4d94ff` (3.8-4.3:1 -> 6.2-7.0:1), six chart pages no longer repeat the template's "Variants" `h2` (a test fails if any docs.md reuses a template-rendered `h2`), main demo x-axis read "Apr Apr Apr" (default tick format kept 3 chars of "Apr 1"). **Evidence:** SSR tests in pre-render mode (no `<style>`, colors in the style attribute); a real SSG build has 0 files matching `<style><!--node-id`; every bar/area/line/arc computes a real color in light and dark; 183 then 209 chart specs green on the SSG build; 294/294 specs on the final tree (8 chart specs, sidebar, navbar, navigation_menu, command, dx-class-migration) with 91/91 navigation_menu links resolving 200; 9 pages x 1280/390 x light/dark with `scrollWidth <= clientWidth`; deploy smoke 80/80 routes carry their hydration script, 0 not-found shells, 0 black/invisible marks. 13 check scripts, fmt, clippy, stylelint green. (Also `8076526`: a clippy `unnecessary_cast` in a carousel test under rustc 1.97, and `.gitignore` now covers every `target-*/`.) **Left open:** rows 120-128 (found in this round; **2026-10-05: 122, 123 and 128 have since LANDED, the rest are open or in this round, see their own rows**). The two classes found here -- dynamic text in raw-text elements, root-absolute internal links -- are closed by their new gates, not by instance fixes. | 118 | shadcn-style `/charts/<kind>/` gallery and a pointer-following tooltip (PR #80) | **LANDED 2026-10-03 -- MentalGear/dioxus-components#80 (merged `437e17b`).** Owner request: model the charts pages on ui.shadcn.com/charts, tooltip included | L | **Measured spec first** (`dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/shadcn-ux-spec.md`; live site probed via curl-routed requests because Chromium cannot load ui.shadcn.com through the sandbox proxy). **Tooltip:** the old one snapped to a fixed anchor per category (no `pointermove`), polar tooltips pinned at the chart centre, nothing clamped to the card, a touch tap left it closed. Now: area/bar/line x snaps to the nearest category (switching at the midpoint), y follows the pointer, box +10px right/below, flips left/above on overflow and clamps inside the chart (pure `place_tooltip`); horizontal bars swap axes; hidden outside the plot; pie anchors at the slice centroid; radar and radial follow the pointer (polar hover resolves from pointer coordinates against resting geometry -- arc `pointerenter` blinked because the active arc's scale moved it under the pointer); 200ms ease-out between positions, no fly-in on (re)open (hidden until measured), off under reduced motion; touch: tap opens and stays, drag scrubs, blur/tap-away closes; keyboard: arrows hang the tooltip off the data point, keyboard focus (not pointer focus) opens the first point like Recharts' `accessibilityLayer`; `default_index` (shadcn `defaultIndex`) renders it open until the user interacts, used by every Tooltips demo; active dot (r=4, series color, card-surface ring) on area/line; a `PointerGate` epoch stops a pointer result that resolves after a close from reopening it (synthetic move+leave stuck open 30/30 before); correct placement under scaled/zoomed ancestors (pointer fraction of the screen rect mapped onto the layout box); `touch-action` keeps pinch-zoom. `fit_width` (opt-in, Cartesian; later superseded by row 119's `height` prop) gave shadcn's `h-[250px] w-full` hero. **Gallery:** `/charts/` and `/charts/<kind>/` (area, bar, line, pie, radar, radial, tooltip), all prerendered: hero with Browse charts / Documentation, a text tab row (`aria-current`, scrolls on phones, keeps its offset and focuses the active tab on switch -- the router scrolled to top and dropped focus to `<body>` before; per-tab document titles), 1/2/3-column grid, each card with shadcn's toolbar (type icon + label, **Copy** = full source to the clipboard, **View Code** = 700px `Sheet`, `Drawer` on phones; Esc/overlay/close return focus). One footer construction for every demo (`CardFooter.dx-chart-footer` > trend + caption; five divergent patterns before, radar's bare divs ran trend and caption together). `area_chart` links its own stylesheet via `AreaChartGallery` instead of a rule mirrored into `main.css`; line interactive's stylesheet was never linked and its ticks read "Apr Apr Apr"; bar interactive totals were black on the dark card (bare `<button>` UA color). **Three fixes that fell out of it:** (1) **client id collision breaking the command palette** -- `use_unique_id` allocated `dxc-{N}` from one process-global counter shared across concurrently rendered SSG pages, so server ids landed anywhere (e.g. `dxc-200..330`) while a client-mounted component counted from 0 in the same namespace; the home page shipped two `id="dxc-229"` and `getElementById` returned the checkbox, so the palette never opened (`command.spec.ts:114`, deterministic on that build, random across builds). **By construction:** client allocations use a disjoint `dxc-c{N}` namespace, server ids unchanged, hydrated components still take the server's exact id via `use_server_cached`; tests cover both shapes and that no client id equals a server id. (2) **scroll-lock horizontal gestures** -- the modal scroll lock's wheel/touchmove handlers looked for a scrollable ancestor on the vertical axis only, so a horizontal swipe in a locked dialog (a wide code block in a bottom drawer) was `preventDefault`ed; the lookup is now axis-aware (dominant of dx/dy, RTL-safe `scrollLeft`); a touch drag moves the code block 0 -> 249px. (3) **copied demo code compiles in a user's app** -- every chart kind's `component.rs` imported `crate::components::chart::component::*`, but after `dx components add` `component` is a private module, so installed charts failed to compile (the repo only compiled because `examples!` made it `pub(crate)`); `examples!` now declares it private like an installed tree, imports use `crate::components::<name>::*` (charts, dialog, popover, label, alert_dialog, command, toast, dashboard email client), proven with a throwaway crate in the installed layout (`cargo check` passes; old path fails); Copy / View Code / CODE tabs show the installed import via one transform that remaps highlight spans, unit-tested over every demo. **Guards by construction:** new gate **`scripts/check-installed-paths.sh`** blocks the `...::component::*` import pattern; the `dxc-c{N}` namespace tests; `charts_gallery.spec.ts` (19 tests incl. clipboard, sheet/drawer focus return, no overflow 1440-320, three axe scans); `chart_tooltip_follow.spec.ts` (22 browser tests) plus placement-math unit tests against shadcn's measured offsets. **Evidence:** 249 chart/gallery specs on an SSG build (tooltip commit), 361/362 targeted specs on the deploy build (the one failure, `navigation_menu.spec.ts:128`, also fails on the previous deploy -- row 121), hero chart box identical before/after hydration, all nine Tooltips demos open at load with no fly-in, tab row stays at 120px across Area->Pie->Radar->Area, deploy 88/88 routes carry their bootstrap script, 0 not-found shells, 0 `<style><!--node-id`; demo sources lost ~570 lines of internal research comments. **Deliberate differences from shadcn:** gallery cards are 0.95x shadcn's width at 1440 (400px vs 419px: the site container is 1280px, shadcn's 1336px, so every 16:9 chart is a uniform 0.95 scale); no "Open in v0" action in the View Code sheet; tooltip position motion is 200ms ease-out (shadcn: 400ms) and never flies in from (0,0); the palette (see row 119). **Left open:** rows 124-126 (pre-hydration jumps, small visual nits). Research: `dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/shadcn-ux-spec.md`, `dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/shadcn-vs-ours.md`. |
| 119 | Recharts/shadcn chart projection parity -- sizing, scales, curves, polar geometry, shadcn-sourced demos, parity tests (PR #81) | **LANDED 2026-10-03 -- MentalGear/dioxus-components#81 (merged `62de3cb`).** Owner report: the gallery's charts projected differently from shadcn's (the big line chart and every radial chart) | L | **Measured against shadcn's rendered SVGs and registry source (`dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/parity-A.md`, `parity-B.md`): the causes were engine semantics, not just demo data.** **Sizing:** a fixed 600x300 viewBox with counter-scaled text shrank strokes, dots and radii and flattened small charts (2:1 vs shadcn's 16:9). Now Recharts `ResponsiveContainer` semantics (`dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/sizing-contract.md`): 1 unit = 1 CSS px, 16:9 for cartesian, a <=250px square for polar, `width`/`height`/`aspect`/`margin` props (`height` replaces `fit_width`); legends reserved inside the box (`legend_size`). **Cartesian:** point x-scale for area/line (first/last on the plot edges), Recharts `getNiceTickValues` (305 -> 0..320, not 0..350), `Curve::Natural` and midpoint `Step`, Recharts bar sizing (`getBarPosition`) with per-corner `BarRadius`, active bar = dashed outline, `cursor` option, `min_tick_gap`/`tick_margin` thinning with preserve-end, Recharts gridline thinning, stack order = JSX order. **Polar:** Recharts angles (degrees, 0 = 3 o'clock, counter-clockwise), `Radius::Px`/`Percent`, radial sweep = value / max x (end - start), background tracks, `RadialGrid` circle grid + spokes, annulus, active slice outer+10 with halo, `PieLabels::Outside` with leader lines, radar nice ticks / anchors / per-series opacity / radius axis / custom ticks, corner fillets fixed. **Load animation** (bars grow, lines/areas reveal, arcs sweep) once after hydration, none under reduced motion (about 169 animations at load, none running after 2.5s, none replayed by hover). **Demos ported from shadcn's registry:** every area/line/bar/pie/radar/radial/tooltip demo now matches its registry source -- the interactive charts use shadcn's fixed 91-row dataset (ours was a synthetic sine wave; totals 24,828 / 25,010), radial Text/Shape/Stacked are partial arcs with shadcn's values (200, 1,260, 1,830), plus datasets, orientation, curves, colors, radii, margins, labels, cursor and tooltip props across ~70 demos; interactive headers use shadcn's layout (bar/line: the card's `display: grid` won on equal specificity and stacked the toggles); gradient areas were painted flat by a CSS `fill` that overrode the `url(#..)` attribute; the legend cards, the tooltip box (128px border-box, was 152px) and the code view (line numbers, not copied) follow. **Root cause class and construction:** demo data/props were hand-typed or invented rather than derived (parity-A "P" items 1-5 were all one class), so by construction the demos are checked against **vendored registry fixtures** (`preview/tests/shadcn/*.json`, extracted by `scripts/extract-shadcn-*fixtures.mjs`) by `preview/src/{chart,polar,chart_tooltip}_parity.rs`, which compare demo data, props and rendered geometry to shadcn's: 24 cartesian charts within 0.006 px; golden tests reproduce shadcn's ticks, curve control points (<0.002 px), bar widths and a measured arc table. Any future change to chart geometry or a demo that drifts from the registry fails `cargo test -p preview`. **Evidence:** 373/374 targeted specs pass on the SSG build (the one failure, `navigation_menu.spec.ts:128`, is pre-existing -- row 121); axe clean on every chart; deploy `scripts/deploy-preview.sh` 88/88 routes carry their bootstrap script, 0 not-found shells, 0 `<style><!--node-id`; gates: 13 check scripts, fmt, clippy, 913 tests, stylelint green. Visual pass (`dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/visual-parity.md`, 1440 light and dark, 390): every card matches except the deliberate/open items below. **Deliberate differences (kept on purpose):** (1) **palette** -- `--dx-chart-1..5` are blue, orange, green, amber and pink while shadcn's current site uses five blues, so series 2+ read orange/green where shadcn's read a darker blue (geometry unaffected); this is the **one deliberate difference left in the gallery** and is resolved by row 111's role tokens (`--chart-1..5`) -- the parity tests must stay green through that migration (row 111's chart tie-in); (2) **stacked radial** splits by the stack total (mobile 0-56 degrees, 570/1830 of the half turn) where Recharts clamps to the largest single value (1260; mobile 0-81 degrees, which clips desktop); (3) **gallery cards 0.95x shadcn width** at 1440 (row 118); (4) **no "Open in v0"** (row 118); (5) **200ms tooltip motion without fly-in** (shadcn 400ms; row 118); (6) **no-JS axis text hidden on fit-height heroes before hydration** (it fades in once the width is measured; the SSR box already has the final height so the marks sit within ~6px of final); (7) interactive x-tick labels start at a different date than shadcn's ("Apr 4" vs "Apr 2", "Apr 6" vs "Apr 3") because the chart is 1230px wide vs 1334px and preserve-end thinning differs -- width-driven, not a bug; (8) Radar and RadialBar keep their full square instead of giving room to the legend (Recharts centres Radar on the whole box). **Left open:** rows 124-125. Research: `dev-docs/research/chart-parity-2026-10-03/` (README indexes all seven reports). |
| 120 | Duplicate `id="dnd-instructions"` on the home page: `DragAndDropList` hard-codes the instructions id | **LANDED 2026-10-05 (round 3)** -- found 2026-10-03 (chart round's full-page DOM scan); actionable when someone next touches `primitives/src/drag_and_drop_list.rs` or a page renders two lists | S | `primitives/src/drag_and_drop_list.rs` sets `aria_describedby: "dnd-instructions"` (~line 450) on the list and `id: "dnd-instructions"` (~line 482) on `DragAndDropInstructions`; the home page gallery renders more than one list, so the document has duplicate ids and every list's `aria-describedby` resolves to the first instance (an a11y defect, invalid HTML). It replaces the per-component RTL variants as the way to SEE RTL (keep them as fixtures for `rtl.spec.ts`) and gives every page an RTL example, which is what shadcn has; (c) a gate `scripts/check-direction-keys.sh`: horizontal arrow-key and gesture handling in `primitives/` must go through `Direction::resolve_horizontal`, or sit on an allowlisted vertical-only case with a stated reason (the shape of the other `check-*.sh` markers); it catches the five holdouts, and must be added to `scripts/run-gates.sh` and to CLAUDE.md's gate sentence; (d) logical-properties CSS: layout needs nothing new, because it already mirrors through CSS logical properties and `check-css-logical-properties.sh` keeps it that way (the toggle in (b) is what would expose a physical-property leak, which then gets fixed in CSS, not worked around per component); (e) an oracle that runs every component's keyboard and positioning smoke under RTL via the toggle, which also answers row 13's open question whether `top_layer.rs`'s JS anchor positioning is direction-aware. **Subsumes:** row 13's follow-ups (`drawer`'s `side` is a physical-geometry fact and `command`'s list is a vertical stack, so the gate's allowlist may record "nothing to flip" for them instead of code), this row's old scope (below) and the shadcn RTL gap. **The `Direction` docs page becomes the explainer for all of it** (what the provider does, that it is reactive, the toggle, the logical-CSS rule, the gate). **Does not cover:** `message_scroller`'s own RTL pass (row 146; it can use the toggle once it exists) and the anchor-positioning audit if (e) is not built. **Old row 137 text (2026-10-04, kept as history):** shadcn's `direction` page documents `DirectionProvider`/`useDirection` (a 6-line re-export of Base UI's).
